I know I keep saying the same thing to you, but... well, that's exactly why I came in here in the first place. I know they aren't just having some petty argument with me. So no, there hasn't been a day where I didn't think about it, because it's just what is happening.\n\nDoctor: Has there ever been a moment, even for a brief instant, where you questioned whether your in-laws were really using the church against you?\n\nPatient: No, not even for a second. Like I said before, I came in here because I know for a fact what they're doing. It's not just a simple argument. They are definitely using the altar and the parish committee to push me out and erase everything I've contributed to this family. I don't question it because I know it's the truth.\n\nDoctor: Do you think other people would agree that your in-laws are using the church against you?\n\nPatient: Well, I'm not sure if other people see it yet. They might be blind to it, or maybe they just don't want to get involved. But I know I keep repeating myself... that's exactly why I came in here to see you. Just this.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe anything else unusual is happening to you besides your in-laws using the church against you?\n\nPatient: Well, there is one other thing. It really only happened during that specific time when I was... well, when I was barely sleeping at all. I was just rushing around everywhere, and I felt absolutely sure that I had this special duty to fix the altar. That's when I heard her, and saw her. \n\nBut then... when my mood dropped later on, I still felt awful—just very guilty and sad—but I wasn't hearing her voice or seeing her anymore. So it was really just during that time when I was so wound up and not sleeping.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt guilty or sad for most of the day since this situation with your in-laws started?\n\nPatient: Yes, I have. Um, after all that energy just sort of left me, I've been waking up with this... this really heavy sadness. It just stays with me for most of the day. It feels like there is no joy left in my house anymore, or... or even in me, really.\n\nDoctor: Have your thoughts about your in-laws using the church against you ever changed or wavered at any point since they started?\n\nPatient: No, they haven't wavered at all. I mean, that's really why I came in here to begin with. I just knew my in-laws were not just arguing with me like families normally do. They were deliberately using the altar and the parish committee to push me out. It's like they want to make everyone completely forget everything I have done for this family. So, no, I've been very sure about what they're doing this whole time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other beliefs like the one about your in-laws using the church against you, that you held to be true despite what others said?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. It's really just been this situation with my in-laws and the church. I haven't had any other... well, I haven't believed anything else like that where people told me I was wrong. It's just this one thing because I know what they're trying to do.\n\nDoctor: Have you heard your late mother's voice when you weren't feeling rushed or anxious?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. Um, hearing her voice and seeing her... that only happened during that time when I was barely sleeping and rushing around everywhere. You know, when I felt so sure I had that special duty to fix the altar. Once all that energy went away and my mood dropped, I still felt guilty and sad, but I stopped hearing or seeing those things. It was really just during that one busy time.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief that your in-laws are using the church against you ever completely gone away, even if just for a short time?\n\nPatient: No, it hasn't gone away. Like I said before, that's the whole reason I'm here. I know they aren't just having normal family disagreements with me. They are actively using the altar and the parish committee to push me out completely. They want to erase me, to make everyone forget everything I've done for this family over the years. So no, I've never stopped believing it, because it's what's happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your in-laws would admit to using the church against you if you asked them directly?\n\nPatient: No, they would never admit it. If I asked them, they'd just say I was crazy or making it up. But I came in here because I know the truth. I know they're not just arguing with me like normal people do. They are actively using the altar and the parish committee to push me out. They want to make everyone forget everything I have done for this family, and they'll never admit to doing it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your in-laws using the church against you could be mistaken?\n\nPatient: No, I'm not mistaken. Like I've been saying, I came in here because I know exactly what they're doing. It's not just regular family arguing. They are deliberately using the altar and the parish committee to push me out completely. They want to erase me and make everyone forget everything I have done for this family over the years. I know what I see, and I'm not wrong about it."
